XII KA AIS KHYNOEW HA RI JAINTIA 

I Kt khyndew Jamtia ha^ng h mem 1833 bad

Ynda ba ka snem 1851^ 16 snem badien pat, ha 
ka nport u Sahep Allen sba ka Sorkar Bengal u 
shem ba don ki khyndew hima ne Raj ba ri lum, 
katto katne na kita la bat da kt rangbah bima kum 
ki jaka sbakri Kum kita ki khyndew ki don ha 
ki 11 tylli ki jaka Doloi Namar bam don nopgpyniaid 
bathikna, hadien la kine ki khyndew la bat da ki Doloi, 
ryngkat bad ki rangbah hima, lane da ki briew ki

bam del hok

nongthew khyndew da ki kbar Bengal ban thew la 
kine ki jaka Ha ki 15 tylli ki ilaka jong ki Doloi 
la shem ba don ki khyndew Raj, ki khyndew jong 
ki Dolot ryngkat bad ki rangbah hima kum ki Pator, 
Basan, Piion, Sangot, Duhalia, nangta ki khyndew

ki Shokidar la ka jingmth ka khyndew, bad katto 
katne ka khyndew ki nongleh puja bad ki shakri 
Syiem la kine ki khyndew ym shym pyrsbang bat 
nc pynlaid da ka Sorkar Phareng.

khajna iing kaba shityngka shi ling kaba la pynkhih
